# Database Tables — Full Column Catalog (Live-Schema Verified)

> Regenerated by direct `INFORMATION_SCHEMA`/`sys.*` introspection against the live development instance (`TOWSIF\MSSQLSERVER2019`, database `SalesDisDB_SMC_NEWDB` — the same instance the application's active `web.config` connection string points at) on the date this file was generated, cross-referenced against actual C# source for every table name to identify callers. This supersedes any prior version of this file — **treat this as ground truth**, not the `spec/database/tables/*.sql` folder (which contains only 1 stray file and is not the real schema source for this project).

**574 tables, 7474 columns, 4 foreign keys, 231 non-PK indexes, 8 unique constraints, 4 check constraints.** (The three `tblOrderPaymentApproval*` tables were added on 2026-08-20 by `deploy_order_payment_approval.sql` and hand-appended below; their columns, indexes, the 4th foreign key and the schema's first trigger are included in the counts above. `tblSAP_SuspectedDuplicateShipment`, 8 columns, and `tblRequsitionChild.WHStockInDetailID`, 1 column, were added on 2026-08-11 — after the live-schema extraction this file was generated from — and hand-appended to the relevant table sections below without a fresh full extraction; see those two entries' own notes. The index/constraint counts above are carried over from the original extraction and not re-verified for the two additions.)

All of the above is `SalesDisDB_SMC_NEWDB` only. A **second, separate database on the same server,
`SAP_API_Data`**, is cross-database queried (`SAP_API_Data..tableName` syntax) by the SAP
stock-receive integration — not introspected as part of this pass, but partially reconstructed from
stored-procedure source and documented in its own [`SAP_API_Data` section](#cross-database-sap_api_data)
near the end of this file. Do not confuse it with the coincidentally-named, apparently-unused local
tables `tblSAP_StockMovementMaster`/`tblSAP_StockMovementDetail` documented in their usual
alphabetical place below — see the callout on those entries.

Caller column reflects a whole-word, case-insensitive scan of `Solution.Web`, `Library.DAL`, `Library.BLL`, `Library.DAO`, `Library.CrystalReports` for the literal table name. It will miss table access that happens only inside a stored procedure body (most access in this codebase — see `spec/database-spec.md`) or via dynamically-built table names — so "no direct caller" here means "not referenced by name directly in C#", not "unused by the system".

## `tblOrder`

Row count (live, at extraction time): **785343**

> **Snapshot columns.** The market-structure columns on this table
> (`GroupId`, `RegionId`, `AreaId`, `TerritoryId`, `SubTerritoryId`, `MarketId`, plus the parallel
> `*Code_Ord` / `*Name_Ord` set) record the hierarchy **as it stood when the order was created** and
> are never back-filled when the structure is reorganized. Do not join or filter them against the
> current `tblGroupInfo` / `tblRegion` / `tblArea` / `tblTerritory` masters expecting agreement —
> see business rule **BR-SI-18** in [`business-rules.md`](business-rules.md).

## `tblOrderPaymentApproval`

Row count (live, at extraction time): **0** (added 2026-08-20 by `deploy_order_payment_approval.sql`; empty after UI/proc test cleanup)

One live approval request per credit-blocked order, driving the AM → DZSM → NSM payment-approval
workflow. `ApprovalStatus` follows the 0–7 code set in `requirements.md`; the approver chain is
snapshotted at request time so a later org-structure change cannot strand an in-flight request.

**Indexes:**

- `IX_tblOrderPaymentApproval_Status` (NONCLUSTERED) on (ApprovalStatus, IsActive) INCLUDE (OrderId, AMEmpId, DZSMEmpId, NSMEmpId)

**Referenced directly in C#:** none by name — all access goes through `sp_OrderPaymentApproval_*`
(called from `Library.DAL\SInventory_DAL\OrderPaymentApprovalRepository.cs`). Also LEFT JOINed by
`sp_LoadOrderListForOrderCreationbyTerri` and `sp_LoadOrderListForOrderRouteDayWise` to expose
`PaymentApprovalStatus` on the Invoice Creation grid.

## `tblOrderPaymentApprovalHistory`

Row count (live, at extraction time): **0** (added 2026-08-20)

Append-only audit trail for the payment-approval workflow. One row per logical state change; a full
approve-through cycle produces 6 rows (Requested, AM Approved, Pending DZSM, DZSM Approved, Pending
NSM, NSM Approved – Fully Approved).

| Column | Type | Nullable | Default | Key |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| HistoryId | int | NOT NULL |  | PK, IDENTITY |
| OrderPaymentApprovalId | int | NOT NULL |  |  |
| OrderId | int | NULL |  |  |
| ActionUserId | int | NULL |  |  |
| ActionEmpId | int | NULL |  |  |
| RoleTypeId | int | NULL |  |  |
| RoleName | nvarchar(50) | NULL |  |  |
| ActionName | nvarchar(50) | NOT NULL |  |  |
| FromStatus | int | NULL |  |  |
| ToStatus | int | NULL |  |  |
| Remarks | nvarchar(500) | NULL |  |  |
| PaymentPlanVersion | int | NULL |  |  |
| OldValue | nvarchar(MAX) | NULL |  |  |
| NewValue | nvarchar(MAX) | NULL |  |  |
| ActionDate | datetime | NOT NULL | GETDATE() |  |

**Indexes:**
- `IX_tblOPAHistory_Approval` (NONCLUSTERED) on (OrderPaymentApprovalId, HistoryId)

**Trigger:** `trg_tblOrderPaymentApprovalHistory_NoChange` — `INSTEAD OF UPDATE, DELETE`, raises
`'tblOrderPaymentApprovalHistory is append-only; UPDATE/DELETE is not permitted.'`. The only table in
this database with such a guard. Note for anyone writing test-cleanup scripts: the trigger must be
explicitly disabled and re-enabled around a delete (see `test_order_payment_approval.ps1`).

**Referenced directly in C#:** none by name — written only by `sp_OrderPaymentApproval_Request` and
`sp_OrderPaymentApproval_Act`, read by `sp_OrderPaymentApproval_GetDetail`.

## `tblOrderPaymentApprovalSchedule`

Row count (live, at extraction time): **0** (added 2026-08-20)

The instalment plan the AM commits the customer to, per plan version. Superseded versions are kept
with `IsActive = 0` rather than deleted.

| Column | Type | Nullable | Default | Key |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| ScheduleId | int | NOT NULL |  | PK, IDENTITY |
| OrderPaymentApprovalId | int | NOT NULL |  | FK → tblOrderPaymentApproval |
| PaymentPlanVersion | int | NOT NULL |  |  |
| PaymentNo | int | NOT NULL |  |  |
| PaymentDate | date | NOT NULL |  |  |
| PaymentAmount | decimal(18,2) | NOT NULL |  | CHECK > 0 |
| EntryBy | int | NULL |  |  |
| EntryDate | datetime | NOT NULL | GETDATE() |  |
| IsActive | bit | NOT NULL | 1 |  |

**Indexes:**
- `UX_tblOPASchedule_Date` (UNIQUE) on (OrderPaymentApprovalId, PaymentPlanVersion, PaymentDate) — duplicate payment dates within one plan version are impossible at the storage layer, not just validated

**Foreign keys:** `FK_tblOPASchedule_Approval` (OrderPaymentApprovalId) → `tblOrderPaymentApproval`.
One of only 4 foreign keys in this database.

**Referenced directly in C#:** none by name — written only by `sp_OrderPaymentApproval_Act`,
read by `sp_OrderPaymentApproval_GetDetail`/`_GetList`.

`WHStockInDetailID` added in `e1d90e0` (2026-08-11), after the last live schema extraction — not reflected in the row count/extraction above. ## `tblSAP_SuspectedDuplicateShipment`

Added in `e1d90e0` (2026-08-11), after the last live schema extraction — no live row count available. See `spec/database/tables/tblSAP_SuspectedDuplicateShipment.sql` and `docs/ReceiveQty_Permanent_Fix_Plan.md` (Problem 2). Written by `sp_SAP_WhStockInMaster` when a Chalan's full product+batch+quantity line set exactly matches an already-processed shipment under a different `ChallanNo`.

## Cross-database: `SAP_API_Data`

**Everything above this line was extracted from `SalesDisDB_SMC_NEWDB`** — the 571-table/7414-column
counts in this file's header do not include what follows. `SAP_API_Data` is a **separate SQL Server
database** on the same instance, reached exclusively via cross-database syntax
(`SAP_API_Data..tableName`) from inside `SalesDisDB_SMC_NEWDB` stored procedures — no live-schema
introspection pass was run directly against it for this file. The schema below is reconstructed from
every `SAP_API_Data..tblSAP_StockMovement*` reference found across `spec/database/procs/*.sql`,
cross-checked against `docs/ReceiveQty_RootCause_Analysis.md` (which ran live, read-only queries
against a dev copy of this database on 2026-08-11, including `sys.indexes`/`sys.columns` checks). This
is **not** a full catalog of `SAP_API_Data` — that database also holds at least `tbl_BankDeposit`
(referenced in `spec/integrations.md` §1) and others not documented here; only the two tables behind
the stock-receive pipeline are covered, matching the scope of this session's investigation.

### `SAP_API_Data..tblSAP_StockMovementMaster`

One row per SAP goods-movement document, filled by an external, out-of-repo SAP-side process (see
`spec/integrations.md` §1). **`StockMovementMasterId` is the only real key** — `challan_code` has
**no unique or index constraint** (confirmed against `sys.indexes` in
`docs/ReceiveQty_RootCause_Analysis.md` §6), so nothing in the database prevents the same physical
shipment being posted more than once under different `challan_code` strings. **This is the confirmed
root cause of a live double-stock-in defect** — the same shipment was posted under two different
`challan_code`s 10 days apart, both were independently processed to completion, and one was received
into stock twice — see `docs/ReceiveQty_RootCause_Analysis.md` §8-§10 and §16 (root cause #1,
severity CRITICAL, confidence CONFIRMED).

| Column | Type (as referenced in procs) | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| StockMovementMasterId | int, surrogate | Effectively the only key; no natural key exists |
| challan_code | nvarchar | **Not unique** — see above |
| challan_date | datetime | The true SAP document date; not always what the UI displays as "Chalan Date" (see root-cause doc §13) |
| is_from_wharehouse | bit | Routes into the warehouse-stock-in leg of the pipeline vs. other legs |
| from_plant_code | nvarchar | |
| to_plant_code | nvarchar | Joined against `tblCompanyUnit.SAP_Code` to resolve `ComUnitId` — **verified directly against the live proc text** (not just the root-cause doc's own, internally inconsistent §7 summary) as present only in `sp_SAP_STOMaster`, `sp_SAP_STODetails`, and `sp_SAP_StockInTransfer`; `sp_SAP_WhStockInMaster`/`sp_SAP_WhStockInDetails` contain no such join at all. Of the three: `sp_SAP_STOMaster` actually writes the resolved `ComUnitId`/`ComUnitCode`/`ComUnitName` into its `INSERT INTO tblRequisition`; `sp_SAP_STODetails` reads them into cursor variables but never writes them to its `INSERT INTO tblRequsitionChild`; `sp_SAP_StockInTransfer` joins `tblCompanyUnit` but doesn't even select its columns — dead computation in the latter two, live in the first |
| truck_no | nvarchar | Written back to `'Ok'` by `MakeRESTRequest` after a live outbound HTTPS call to SAP (see `spec/integrations.md` §1a-revised) |
| driver_name | nvarchar | |
| entryDate | datetime | |
| IsReceived | bit | Set by `sp_SAP_StoNoforChallanConfirmDone` |
| ReceiveDate | datetime | Set by `sp_SAP_StoNoforChallanConfirmDone` |
| ReceiveBy | int | Written by `MakeRESTRequest` (`@RcvBy` parameter) |
| isConfirmDone | bit | Set by `sp_SAP_StoNoforChallanConfirmDone` |

No movement-type, SAP document number, or line-item number column exists on this table at all —
every procedure in the pipeline matches purely on `challan_code` string equality (root-cause doc §11).

**Read (cross-DB) by:** `sp_SAP_WhStockInMaster`, `sp_SAP_WhStockInDetails`, `sp_SAP_STOMaster`,
`sp_SAP_STODetails`, `sp_SAP_StockInTransfer`, `sp_Get_SAP_StockReceivePendingData`,
`sp_Get_SAP_StockReceivePendingDataById`, `sp_Get_SAP_DICStockReceivePendingData`,
`sp_Get_SAP_HideChallanByChallanNo`, `sp_SAP_StoNoforChallanConfirm`, `sp_SAP_B2BChallanMasterInsert`,
`sp_SAP_B2BChallanDetailInsert`, `sp_SAP_RequisitionMasterUpdate`, `sp_SAP_RequisitionDetailUpdate`,
`MakeRESTRequest`, and `sp_SAP_StockReceive` (the orchestrator that chains most of the above — see
`docs/ReceiveQty_RootCause_Analysis.md` §3 for the full call graph, triggered manually from
`Solution.Web/SAP_Integration/SAP_StockReceive.aspx` via
`SAP_IntrigationPointDAL.SaveStockReceive`).

**Written (cross-DB) by:** `sp_SAP_StoNoforChallanConfirmDone` (`isConfirmDone`, `IsReceived`,
`ReceiveDate`) and `MakeRESTRequest` (`truck_no`, `ReceiveBy`). `sp_SAP_Save_StockMovementMaster` also
`INSERT`s full rows into this table, but has **no confirmed C# caller** (listed as orphaned in
`spec/database-spec.md`'s Orphan Inventory) — the active writer of new rows in practice is the
external SAP-side process per `spec/integrations.md` §1, not this procedure.

---

### `SAP_API_Data..tblSAP_StockMovementDetail`

One row per product/batch/quantity line under a `tblSAP_StockMovementMaster` row, joined
`M.StockMovementMasterId = D.StockMovementMasterId` (a correct, non-multiplying FK-style join — see
root-cause doc §7).

| Column | Type (as referenced in procs) | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| StockMovementDetailId | int | PK |
| StockMovementMasterId | int | FK by convention only — **no FK constraint found** (root-cause doc §6) |
| product_code | nvarchar | |
| batch_no | nvarchar | Kept as a distinguishing column throughout the pipeline — batches are not incorrectly merged (root-cause doc §12) |
| quantity | decimal | Updatable post-hoc by staff — see `sp_SAP_Up_StockReceiveQty` in the write list below |
| unit_price | decimal | |
| UOM | nvarchar | |
| unit_vat | decimal | |
| net_amount | decimal | |
| expiry_date | datetime | |
| manufacturer_date | datetime | |
| Original_SAP_Stock | decimal | Written equal to `quantity` at insert time by `sp_SAP_Save_StockMovementDetails` (same orphaned-procedure caveat as the Master table's INSERT counterpart above) |

**Read (cross-DB) by:** the same procedure set as `tblSAP_StockMovementMaster` above (the two tables
are always joined together), most consequentially `sp_SAP_WhStockInDetails`, which produces **one
output row per matching Master×Detail row with no `GROUP BY`/`SUM`** — this is the exact point in the
pipeline where a SAP-side duplicate or inflated-quantity row would flow straight into
`tblWHStockInDetail` unfiltered (root-cause doc §5, §10).

**Written (cross-DB) by:** `sp_SAP_Save_StockMovementDetails` (`INSERT`s full rows, including
`Original_SAP_Stock = @quantity`; same no-confirmed-C#-caller caveat as its Master-table counterpart)
and **`sp_SAP_Up_StockReceiveQty`** (`UPDATE ... SET quantity = @quantity WHERE
StockMovementDetailId = @StockMovementDetailId`) — the latter **does** have a confirmed C# caller:
`SAP_IntrigationPointDAL.UpdateSAPQtyStockReceive`, invoked from
`Solution.Web/SAP_Integration/SAP_StockReceive.aspx.cs` and `SAP_StockReceiveDIC.aspx.cs`, i.e. staff
can directly edit a pending SAP detail row's `quantity` from the admin UI before it ever reaches
`tblWHStockInDetail`.

---
